================================================================================
5. PHASE 7 — GEOGRAPHIC PRICE / m² INTELLIGENCE
================================================================================
Phase7 uses geographic-scope, geographic-distribution, geographic-statistics and geographic-conclusions over acquired observations. Ordinary engine and Phase11 analyzer consume them; Step11 geographic adapter reuses B1 observations plus selected-market distribution, selecting the immediate child level. ================================================================================
6. PHASE 8 — SIZE → PRICE / m²
================================================================================
Phase8 size-relationship-population builds exact-area band observations and represented n. size-relationship-math calculates band-coordinate relationships (Spearman, log-log regression, R² and modeled change). size-relationship.ts is its contract. Ordinary and Phase11 callers reuse this machinery; Step11 size adapter calls lower-level population/math directly with B1 observations. No new acquisition in the adapter. Populated bands are not invented exact individual measurements; no range midpoint is used as an exact observation in this path.

Exact construction/property areas support the ratio; ratio eligibility and regression withholding belong to Phase9. Ordinary analysis acquires no additional evidence here. Step11 calls lower-level identity/population/statistics/relationship rather than the top-level acquisition engine; it reuses B1 observations and established subject ratio evidence.

================================================================================
8. PHASE 10 — CHARACTERISTIC PRICE / m² COMPARISON
================================================================================
Standalone Phase10: comparison controls -> executePriceMeterComparison (Server Action) -> entitlement -> options/request parser -> single-use comparison permit -> getPriceMeterComparisonAnalysis -> loadPriceMeterComparisonCandidates -> FX/observations/cohort -> buildPriceMeterComparison -> completed server-rendered EN/ES results.
Candidate acquisition resolves both A/B boundaries before listing reads. It loads canonical geographic membership for each distinct geography, then required property-type/characteristic memberships over bounded IDs, intersects per cohort, fetches union listing rows for active requested transaction, hydrates canonical evidence, and computes independent A/B populations. Membership anchor queries filter canonical version but not active/transaction; those restrictions arrive at listing retrieval. Area/ratio restrictions are evaluated after acquisition. This is PARTIAL early bounding, not an unbounded all-country engine.
Step11 bypasses this top-level engine and loader: its joint acquisition planner supplies rows/memberships to the lower-level comparison orchestrator. No independent A/B population or methodological identity is merged by acquisition reuse.

================================================================================
9. PHASE 11 — CROSS-DIMENSIONAL PRICE / m² ANALYSIS
================================================================================
PRE-14-1 path: client PriceMeterCrossDimensionalAnalysis -> /api/price-meter/cross-dimensional POST -> entitlement and bounded Apply grammar -> question authority -> one observation acquisition -> transaction/basis/cohort -> analyzer -> outcomes -> synthesis/invariants -> projectCompletedResult -> type-only result contract -> client result renderer.
Question, outcomes and synthesis now import server-only. Client presentation consumes completed results; it does not compute outcomes or synthesis. DTO projection is IN the route (projectCompletedResult), not a separate invented DTO module.
Question grammar only selects an authorized owning relationship/secondary dimension. Secondary cohorts partition acquired evidence; no powerset acquisition. The route intentionally reacquires the selected market for this independent request; the preceding ordinary Apply result is not a server cache.
Current source verifier: scripts/verification/pre14-phase11-boundary.cjs; not executed in this manifest task. Current import graph is separately re-inspected.

================================================================================
10. PHASE 12 — PROPERTY PRICE / m² POSITION
================================================================================
B1 production trace (Sale and Rent, EN and ES): listing page -> PriceMeterPropertyPositionListing -> config GET /api/price-meter/property-position -> authorization -> loadPositionSubject -> canonical hydration -> analysis date/optional subject FX -> configuration. Where District is established, the mounted client separately POSTs default District/normalization analysis. Missing District does not silently fall back; supported explicit scope changes can select another known scope.
POST: strict PositionRequest -> authorization -> executePropertyPosition -> executePropertyPositionWithWorkingEvidence -> canonical active subject -> explicit reference {geography, property type, transaction, basis, normalization, SUBJECT_INCLUDED, date/monetary policy} -> loadPositionReference -> complete canonical acquisition -> same-date FX -> observations -> explicit consistent subject participation -> distribution/neutral positioning adapters -> internal result -> toPositionDTO -> bilingual evidence.
Loader pages 500 rows with exact count/stability checks and 25-ID listing batches. Geographic IDs are established first; property-type membership bounds listing retrieval; hydrated evidence is checked against the reference. Subject fetch is bounded to one identity. Exact area is required for normalization, not imputed from ranges.
Execution file owns server-private working evidence. Public server wrapper returns only result. Position DTO excludes rows, observation lists and internal causes. B1 reference selection is separate from Phase12A comparability.
GET configuration and subsequent POST are distinct acquisitions. Step11 commits execute a new B1 computation within their own request rather than reusing a browser-carried prior result.

================================================================================
11. PHASE 12 STEP 11 — PROPERTY PRICE / m² DIFFERENCE CONTEXT
================================================================================
Step11 path: PriceMeterPropertyPositionListing -> PriceMeterPropertyDifferenceContext (draft selections) -> explicit onCommit -> POST /api/price-meter/property-difference-context -> executeDifferenceContext -> authorization -> parseDifferenceRequest -> executePropertyPositionWithWorkingEvidence once -> selected adapters -> toDifferenceDTO -> independent bilingual context cards alongside the newly computed position.
B1 envelope retains subjectRow, reference rows, canonical observations and distribution in a local returned object. Step11's EstablishedExecution and local Maps reuse them while this one awaited request executes. No persistent cache, database storage or browser exposure of the working envelope was found. Browser may receive projected results and option metadata, not temporary acquisition state.
Geographic/size/C:L adapters perform zero new reads. Characteristic selections first resolve options and A/B definitions, reject resolved duplicates, then acquire their union: same reference geography/type reuses B1 candidate IDs; other selected geographies query memberships once per unique geography; membership reads are batched over shared IDs/required terms; only missing eligible rows are fetched/hydrated; B1 observations are reused. Existing B1 FX is reused, or additional USD evidence resolves FX using the SAME analytical date. Cohort populations remain independent.
Operational controls: MAX_CONTEXT_QUESTIONS=7; MAX_CHARACTERISTIC_COMPARISONS=4; one geography family, one size family and one C:L family; duplicate raw comparison intent and resolved cohort identity rejected. These are implementation controls, not analytical invariants.
Empty selection: B1 still executes once; zero contextual questions perform zero contextual analytical/acquisition work; resolveComparisons exits without options I/O. No execute-everything branch or contextual powerset.
Stale response protection: generation ticket on position/context requests; reference changes clear prior context and remount by listing:geography:normalization key. Initial B1 config/analysis promises are retained per mounted listing to resist StrictMode effect replay. This does not guarantee transport/server cancellation for obsolete requests.
No combined synthesis, contribution arithmetic or causal decomposition. Context status/provenance, represented n and phase-owned methodology remain independent.

================================================================================
12. PHASE 12A — USER-DEFINED COMPARABLE COHORT
================================================================================
Phase12A is not Phase14 infrastructure by inheritance. Listing hosts mount PriceMeterComparableListing -> /api/comparable GET configuration / POST explicit request -> comparable-server authorization -> comparable-loader -> engine -> DTO -> browser.
Subject identity and immutable transaction/property basis/normalization boundaries originate from the subject; compulsory property type and applicable property/construction area cohorts are resolved in subject-identity/base-cohort. Optional dimensions in comparable-dimensions: bedrooms, bathrooms, parking, year_built, construction_land, environment, terrain, utility, accessibility, legal_status.
Canonical subject path: explicit geography membership bounds peers (subject excluded in candidate SQL); complete batched membership acquisition determines property-type candidates; bounded listing reads with area predicates; canonical hydration; subject FX reused or same-date candidate FX obtained; observations and subject membership supplied to engine. Engine builds deterministic active-dimension intersection and cumulative population trail; final peer positioning excludes subject. Shared math owns arithmetic, not peer rules. Canonical exact year-built evidence is handled separately from unestablished year evidence.
Legacy subject branch still exists: text geography/property type and unpaginated bounded listing query. It is discriminator-isolated (.is canonical_domain_version null), not a canonical-record fallback. No live legacy population presence was queried. Record as retained compatibility and conditional completeness risk, not current canonical identity authority.
Current actual browser contract is lib/price-meter-comparable-browser-contract.ts. Root duplicate is unused and stale.

================================================================================
13. PHASE 14 — COMPARATIVE PRICE / m² DISCOVERY
================================================================================
FOUNDATION EXISTS; PRODUCTION DISCOVERY PRODUCT DOES NOT.
Five lib/price-meter-discovery-* files exist. No application import, HTTP route, Server Action or verification reference invokes runPriceMeterDiscovery or loadPriceMeterDiscoveryBoundedPopulation in the scanned app/lib/scripts source. The loader and engine are separate unconnected entry functions; request/population/criterion only connect inside this dormant cluster.
Per-file detailed classification:
- request: DORMANT + STALE SCALE ASSUMPTION. Requires Province/Canton/District, transaction, property type/basis/normalization, Property Area and applicable Construction Area bands plus a distribution criterion. Not server-only-poisoned. No DB. No Phase12A subject/peer dependency.
- loader: DORMANT + STALE GEOGRAPHY/ACQUISITION. server-only. SQL filters active transaction and TEXT province/canton plus area; unpaginated listing read; full geography dictionary via resolve-listing-geography; text-based resolution; unchunked/unpaginated membership IN; optional BCCR FX. No canonical-domain discriminator or hydrateCanonicalPopulation. No caller. No entitlement gate of its own.
- population: DORMANT + STALE SCALE ASSUMPTION. In-memory Canton population and contained District subset from supplied observations/memberships; property/area compatibility. No own server-only marker. No DB or Phase12A contract.
- criterion: DORMANT. Pure criterion boundary/qualification over supplied distribution; no ranking, query or subject. No own server-only marker.
- engine: DORMANT + STALE SCALE ASSUMPTION. Runs both District and Canton distributions/qualification, returns both observation arrays/counts. No DB or own server-only marker. No score/rank, recursive expansion, minimum-n fallback or candidate powerset.
The old code's mandatory dual-geography model must NOT be promoted to current Phase14 law. Its fields and comments saying canonical do not confer approval. No subject-required/Phase12A-peer-baseline discovery implementation was found in these five files; the stale implementation differs from the stale DOCUMENT premise.
Generic reusable identity, distribution, canonical population readers, permits and request-scoped B1 reuse are generic infrastructure, not active Phase14 product integration.

================================================================================
14. SUPABASE / DATABASE / EVIDENCE ACCESS
================================================================================
DIRECT versus DELEGATING: each record lists actual table/RPC call sites and separate lower-level dependency. A module import does not prove every co-located query executes on a PPM2 request. Storage getPublicUrl in resolveListingImages constructs URLs and is NOT database/evidence acquisition.
Canonical reads: statistics-engine membership/listing SELECT -> canonical-population -> canonical-listing-reader RPC read_canonical_listing_evidence, 25 IDs per RPC. Canonical geography resolution uses ontology_terms plus bounded official-code/ancestor resolution. Legacy paths use read_legacy_geographic_candidates/read_legacy_geography_dictionary and isolated legacy text semantics.
Membership reads: price-meter-ontology-membership once per union of ordinary selected observation IDs, 25 IDs/1500 encoded-character chunks, exact 500-row pages, characteristic-type restriction. Comparable/comparison/position/Step11 loaders have their own bounded pipelines described above.
Ancillary reads: explorer-options-engine ontology options cached 86400 seconds; shared listing hosts read public listing/ontology display evidence; authorization RPC reads entitlement state before analytics.
FX: registry SELECT and conditional INSERT; BCCR external fetch. Saved results: saved-analyses INSERT/SELECT/UPDATE/DELETE; load-analysis owned SELECT; account-storage records reopen activity. None implies re-running the analytical engine during save itself.
Statistics cache writes and calculateDistribution in statistics-engine are not called by current getPriceMeterAnalysis. Dormant discovery has its own unsafe-completeness pattern. Wire request totals are not inferred from source call-site counts.

================================================================================
15. CANONICAL GEOGRAPHY CONSUMPTION
================================================================================
Current canonical path: request official code/slug -> resolvePopulationGeography -> resolveDtaGeography -> lossless canonical IDs/hierarchy -> listing memberships -> complete bounded listing identities -> readCanonicalListingEvidence -> hydrateCanonicalPopulation -> observation canonicalGeography. Supplied geographic dimensions intersect; authorized multiple District values are OR within their dimension. No new PPM2 hierarchy is defined for canonical records.
B1 and Step11 use canonical geographic IDs from hydrated evidence; Phase12A canonical branch also uses memberships. Phase10 uses selected canonical option terms and canonical listing membership.
Legacy exception: observation-loader resolves only noncanonical rows via legacy dictionary. Phase12A has a discriminator-isolated legacy subject path. Dormant discovery does NOT isolate legacy or hydrate canonical evidence and instead resolves text; this is a stale canonical/implementation contradiction if activated.
Losslessness: dta-identity and canonical reader use string IDs. Phase10 parser/Step11 comparison options still have numeric term contracts; Step11 safeTerm and contextOptions reject unsafe integers rather than silently accept them. Ordinary characteristic membership and dormant discovery use numeric IDs without equivalent universal lossless protection. Their suitability for larger ontology IDs is not established by current types.

================================================================================
16. FX / MONETARY PATHS
================================================================================
Date authority: analysis-date.ts uses America/Costa_Rica civil date, not listing time or FX effective date. PPM2 identity preserves original currency plus analytical CRC and BCCR reference-sale provenance, requested date and actual effective date.
FX service: exact registry lookup -> if absent BCCR indicator318 exact-date fetch -> registry insert/check -> retry exact; otherwise 10-day historical backfill -> exact/prior lookup. BCCR fetch uses no-store. This is runtime write capability within an analytical call; not repaired.
No active hardcoded PPM2 conversion rate found. Numbers such as 500 page size and 1500 request budget are not FX. The debug saved-analysis-integrity route contains a historical fixture rate 451.28, not a market conversion rule. That route can call persistence helpers; it was not run.
Reuse is request-local in B1/Step11 and Phase12A subject/candidate FX. Independent config/analysis/ordinary/Phase11/Step11 calls can repeat registry reads. No cross-request analytical evidence cache was found; the persistent FX registry and cached ontology options are separate facilities.

================================================================================
17. AUTHORIZATION PATHS
================================================================================
Major protected paths all authorize BEFORE analytical population acquisition:
- ordinary Apply: executePriceMeterApply -> authorizePriceMeterIntelligenceExecution before options/loader/engine;
- Phase10: executePriceMeterComparison before options/candidates;
- Phase11: POST cross-dimensional authorization before observation-loader;
- Phase12: GET/POST property-position route authorization before configuration/execution;
- Phase12A: comparable-server authorizes before subject/candidate loader;
- Step11: GET authorizes options; POST coordinator authorizes before B1/acquisition.
Gate: supabase-server auth.getUser then exact RPC current_user_has_entitlement('price-m2-intelligence'), strict true result. Apply/comparison permits enforce committed execution intent, not entitlement by themselves.
PermissionGate/permissions provide UI visibility only. Public listing-page display reads and shell option reads can precede analytical authorization by design; distinguish these from protected Crown Jewel population acquisition.
Dormant discovery loader has no entry-point authorization. No product caller currently exposes it. Any future wiring requires explicit authorized boundary; this manifest does not design one.
Diagnostic routes and saved-result CRUD are separately mapped, not represented as entitlement-gated analytical engines. Deployed grants/RLS were not re-queried.


================================================================================
18. APPLICATION ENTRY POINTS
================================================================================
Application roots and route edges:
INTELLIGENCE HUB: app/en/market-intelligence/page.tsx -> MarketIntelligenceTabs; app/es/inteligencia-de-mercado/page.tsx -> PestanasInteligenciaMercado -> shared PriceMeterApplyPanel.
MARKET HUB: app/en/market-hub/page.tsx and app/es/centro-de-mercado/page.tsx -> shared workspace/tab presentation. Workspace price-meter branch does not invoke analytics on page rendering.
PUBLIC STANDALONE SHELL: app/price-per-square-meter/page.tsx (EN), app/es/precio-por-metro-cuadrado/page.tsx (ES); shared explicit Apply and A/B action controls.
LISTING PAGE: app/en/buy/listing/[id]/page.tsx, app/en/rent-lease/listing/[id]/page.tsx, app/es/comprar/anuncio/[id]/page.tsx, app/es/alquilar-arrendar/anuncio/[id]/page.tsx mount Phase12+Step11 and independent Phase12A clients for Sale/Rent.
API ROUTES: /api/comparable; /api/price-meter/cross-dimensional; /api/price-meter/property-position; /api/price-meter/property-difference-context.
SERVER ACTIONS: lib/price-meter-apply-action.ts and lib/price-meter-comparison-action.tsx.
OWNED SAVED ANALYSIS PAGES: EN saved-analysis/[id], ES analisis-guardado/[id], restore filters and redirect; no automatic PPM2 recalculation.
OTHER / DIAGNOSTIC: /api/debug/bccr-fx and /api/debug/saved-analysis-integrity. They are route-reachable diagnostics, not hidden just because named debug. No requests were sent.
Each application record includes actual imported modules, fetch literals and callers. An absence of 'use client' in a file does not imply server-only if a client root imports it.

25. STALE / SUPERSEDED ARCHITECTURAL ASSUMPTIONS
================================================================================
CANONICAL / IMPLEMENTATION CONTRADICTIONS:
1. Geographic populations must derive from canonical membership, without text fallback. Dormant discovery-loader.ts instead bounds province/canton by term_name text and reconstructs canonicalGeography from legacy fields without a canonical discriminator or reader hydration. Reachability: dormant. Consequence: omissions/misclassification and noncanonical evidence if wired unchanged; unpaginated listing and membership responses can truncate. Blocks reuse of this loader as approved Phase14 acquisition, not further architecture reconnaissance.
2. Phase14 scale remains unresolved. Dormant discovery-request/population/engine require District plus Canton and automatically calculate both distributions. No insufficiency fallback: both are explicit old contract outputs. Consequence: fixed fan-out and scale assumptions cannot be inherited as approved architecture. No repair selected.
3. Synthetic confidence retired: root duplicate comparable browser contract still declares a confidence object. Unimported/type-only: stale contract, not an active producer. Active lib confidence modules are deleted; current production PPM2 pipeline has no synthetic confidence producer found.
No Phase12A-subject/peer-baseline discovery implementation was found. Do not misclassify legitimate subject-relative Phase12/12A/Step11 code as discovery.
No active PPM2 range midpoint imputation, Sale/Rent cohort mixing, C:L-as-Site-Coverage rule, active confidence score, client analytical import or filter powerset was established in inspected paths. Missing/unknown Property Basis is excluded by eligible analytical cohort construction; independent transaction cohorts are not mixed.
Retained legacy population branches are labeled compatibility. Their existence is not proof of current live legacy data, nor permission to reuse text geography for canonical records.


================================================================================
26. EGRESS / CPU / ACQUISITION RISK MAP
================================================================================
ACQUISITION PATHS / BOUND-FIRST:
A ordinary: validated Apply geographic/transaction boundary -> getMatchingListings -> exact 500-row pages, 25-ID/1500-encoded-length batches -> canonical RPC hydration -> observations and shared membership -> in-memory analyses. PARTIAL: geography bounds before listing acquisition, but area/distance exact filtering follows hydration; legacy compatibility path also executes. No getMarketStatistics x10 path.
B Phase7: same ordinary/B1/Phase11 acquired boundary -> in-memory geographic partitions/distributions/statistics. YES within owning bounded request; no new acquisition.
C Phase8: owning bounded observations -> exact size bands -> relationship math. YES; no new acquisition.
D Phase9: owning bounded exact evidence -> C:L cohorts/statistics/relationships. YES; no new acquisition.
E Phase10: two committed cohort geographies -> candidate loader -> batched memberships/listings -> hydration -> FX/observations -> A/B. PARTIAL: geographic membership scan includes statuses/transactions before later active/transaction listing restriction; area/ratio postfiltering.
F Phase11: authorized one-question/Apply boundary -> ordinary observation loader -> complete acquisition/hydration -> partitions -> Phase7/8/9 method -> outcomes/synthesis. PARTIAL inherits ordinary late filters; no per-secondary-cohort DB loading found.
G Phase12: authorized subject ID -> subject reader/hydration -> explicit reference -> membership/type bounded pages -> listing hydration -> FX -> observations/participation -> position. YES for geographic/type acquisition; pagination is not a runtime population ceiling.
H Phase12A: authorized subject -> geography/property type/area boundary -> complete canonical membership/batched listings -> hydration -> observations -> peers/exclusion/position. YES canonical geographic bounding, with optional peer dimensions evaluated in memory; PARTIAL for retained legacy unpaginated listing branch.
I Step11: selected bounded intent -> B1 once -> selected-context planner -> extra selected comparisons only -> missing-row hydration/FX -> independent lower-level methods. YES for committed selected-question boundaries; characteristic memberships precede remaining exact area/ratio exclusions. No unrequested contextual work.
J dormant discovery: required Canton/area text bounds -> one unpaginated listing read -> full geography dictionary and unchunked memberships -> observations -> dual District/Canton calculations. PARTIAL as a historical SQL bound; NOT an approved canonical bound-first path.

STATIC RISK CLASSIFICATIONS:
CURRENT ACTIVE RISK: separate B1 config/default analysis repeats subject hydration; a later Step11 commit reacquires B1 reference evidence; a Phase11 request reacquires the ordinary market. These are distinct request paths, not a proven one-click multiplier. No cache eliminates them.

CURRENTLY BOUNDED: complete pages/ID batches; ordinary membership loaded once for all basis cohorts; selected Step11 comparisons share geography/membership/row acquisition. Pages can be numerous as the selected population grows; pagination is not a total workload cap.
CURRENTLY BOUNDED: characteristic distributions enumerate encountered memberships/basis lenses in memory. This is fixed ordinary feature work, not a filter powerset or automatic Step11 context selection. Phase7 may build observed geographic groups in memory; it does not refetch all geographic levels.
CURRENT ACTIVE RISK: canonical memberships used for initial filtering can overlap property-type/geography evidence later returned by canonical hydration. Step11 selected membership reads may repeat evidence on B1 IDs while reusing rows. Exact egress requires measurements.
DORMANT RISK: old discovery unpaginated queries, giant IN list, full geography dictionary and mandatory dual distributions. Missing completeness is not a safe population cap.
CURRENTLY BOUNDED / PRESENTATION: initial B1 promise reuse resists same-mount effect replay; generation IDs suppress stale displays; normal Apply and comparison use explicit controls. Browser abort/stale suppression does not prove database cancellation. Actual duplicate requests/SSR-CSR multiplication NOT ESTABLISHED WITHOUT LIVE MEASUREMENT.
PHASE14-SPECIFIC RISK: activating dormant foundation would inherit stale scale, dictionary, hydration/completeness and missing entitlement-entry assumptions. Do not infer safe Canton/country execution.
No active or dormant filter powerset, recursive minimum-n broadening, speculative every-context acquisition or candidate ranking precomputation found in traced PPM2 sources.
